- **agent-doctor**: Diagnose and fix Copilot / VS Code agent files (`*.agent.md`) — especially their `tools:` list — and keep them correct as plugins install and MCP servers change. - **agent-evals**: 为 LLM/agent 应用建立回归评估：从真实轨迹做错误分析、按失败类型建金标准用例、先写确定性评分器再用经过校准的 LLM 评审、多次试验看置信区间、和基线对比并在 CI 里拦截回退。Use when building or changing an agent/LLM app (prompts plus tools, retrieval, memory, model swap) and you need evidence it got better, when asked to 做评估集、eval harness、LLM-as-judge、回归测试 agent, or before switching models. 只改一段可复用 prompt 用 prompt-optimizer；评估技能改动用 writing-skills；普通代码单元测试用 test-driven-development。 ([haxsd/agent-skills](https://github.com/haxsd/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-evals))
- **agent-evals**: Design, build, and maintain evaluations for AI agents — task sets, graders, eval harnesses, and metrics like pass@k and pass^k. Use this skill whenever the user mentions evals, evaluation suites, benchmarks, LLM-as-judge, graders, regression tests for an agent, "how do I know if my agent got better/worse", agent quality measurement, or is about to ship, tune, or upgrade the model behind an agent — even if they don't use the word "eval". Also use it when the user is writing a spec for a new agent feature, since eval tasks are the sharpest way to make a spec concrete. ([IamGianluca/dotfiles](https://github.com/IamGianluca/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-evals))
- **agent-evals**: Golden-task evaluation harness for the agent fleet. For each golden task it runs an agent persona (a gpt-4o-class model, credit-funded, OpenAI-direct by default) to produce an answer, scores it with an LLM-as-judge against an explicit rubric, prints a scorecard, and (with --emit) sends eval_result events to the PostHog Fleet Agents project. Use to measure agent quality, gate it in CI, and catch quality regressions across roles (CTO/CFO/CLO). Part of Fleet Intelligence ([InnerScopeHearing/otchealth-claude-tools](https://github.com/InnerScopeHearing/otchealth-claude-tools/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-evals))
